## Call 1

```python
import numpy as np
print(sorted(W.keys()))
for l in range(4):
    print("Layer",l)
    for n in ['ln1_w','ln1_b','W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o','ln2_w','ln2_b','W_fc','W_proj']:
        a=W[f'{n}.{l}']
        s=f"{n}: shape {a.shape} mean {a.mean():.4f} std {a.std():.4f} max|{np.abs(a).max():.4f}"
        if a.ndim==2:
            sv=np.linalg.svd(a,compute_uv=False)
            s+=f" sv top {sv[:5].round(2)} median {np.median(sv):.3f} min {sv[-1]:.4f}"
        print(s)
    # per-head norms
    for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        a=W[f'{n}.{l}']
        print(n,'head fro',[round(float(np.linalg.norm(a[64*j:64*j+64])),2) for j in range(6)])
    a=W[f'W_o.{l}']
    print('W_o head fro',[round(float(np.linalg.norm(a[:,64*j:64*j+64])),2) for j in range(6)])

```

    for n in ['ln1_w','ln2_w']:

## Call 3

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=3,suppress=True)
d=384
# coordinate-level usage
def cn(A): return np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
def rn(A): return np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1)
w0=W['ln1_w.0']; w2=W['ln2_w.0']
print('corr ln1_w.0 with col norms: q,k,v,fc', [round(float(np.corrcoef(w0,cn(W[n]))[0,1]),3) for n in ['W_q.0','W_k.0','W_v.0']], round(float(np.corrcoef(w2,cn(W['W_fc.0']))[0,1]),3))
idx=np.argsort(-w0)[:20]
print('top ln1_w.0 idx',idx, w0[idx].round(2))
for n in ['W_q.0','W_k.0','W_v.0','W_fc.0']:
    print(n,'col-norm at top idx',cn(W[n])[idx].round(2),'median col',np.median(cn(W[n])).round(2))
for l in range(4):
    for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_fc']:
        c=cn(W[f'{n}.{l}']); o=np.argsort(-c)[:8]
        print(n,l,'top col idx',o,'norm',c[o].round(2),'med',np.median(c).round(2))
    for n in ['W_o','W_proj']:
        c=rn(W[f'{n}.{l}']); o=np.argsort(-c)[:8]
        print(n,l,'top ROW idx',o,'norm',c[o].round(2),'med',np.median(c).round(2))

```

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
special=[273,123,21,307,146,51,125,35,86,87,17,360]
print('special',special)
for l in range(4):
    print('LAYER',l)
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        print(f' H{j} W_o row norm at special',np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_o.{l}"][:,s][special],axis=1).round(2),' median',np.median(np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_o.{l}"][:,s],axis=1)).round(2))
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        print(f' H{j} W_k col norm at special',np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_k.{l}"][s][:,special],axis=0).round(2),' median',np.median(np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_k.{l}"][s],axis=0)).round(2))
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        print(f' H{j} W_q col norm at special',np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_q.{l}"][s][:,special],axis=0).round(2),' median',np.median(np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_q.{l}"][s],axis=0)).round(2))
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        print(f' H{j} W_v col norm at special',np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_v.{l}"][s][:,special],axis=0).round(2),' median',np.median(np.linalg.norm(W[f"W_v.{l}"][s],axis=0)).round(2))
    print(' ln1_w at special',W[f'ln1_w.{l}'][special].round(2),' ln1_b',W[f'ln1_b.{l}'][special].round(2))
    print(' ln2_w at special',W[f'ln2_w.{l}'][special].round(2),' ln2_b',W[f'ln2_b.{l}'][special].round(2))
    print(' W_fc col norm special',np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_fc.{l}'][:,special],axis=0).round(2),' proj row norm',np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_proj.{l}'][special],axis=1).round(2))

## Call 5

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
D=W['ln1_w.0']
for j in range(6):
    s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
    Q=W['W_q.0'][s]; K=W['W_k.0'][s]; V=W['W_v.0'][s]; O=W['W_o.0'][:,s]
    print('H',j)
    print(' Q sv',np.linalg.svd(Q,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    print(' K sv',np.linalg.svd(K,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    print(' V sv',np.linalg.svd(V,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    print(' O sv',np.linalg.svd(O,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    QD=Q*D; KD=K*D
    print(' QD sv',np.linalg.svd(QD,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    print(' KD sv',np.linalg.svd(KD,compute_uv=False)[:64:3].round(2))
    B=(QD.T@KD)/8
    print(' B sv',np.linalg.svd(B,compute_uv=False)[:40:2].round(2),' fro',np.linalg.norm(B).round(2),' sym frac',(np.linalg.norm((B+B.T)/2)/np.linalg.norm(B)).round(3),' trace',np.trace(B).round(2))
    A=(O@(V*D))
    print(' OVD sv',np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)[:30:2].round(2))
# stacked subspace
for name,A in [('qkv',np.vstack([W['W_q.0'],W['W_k.0'],W['W_v.0']])),('qkvD',np.vstack([W['W_q.0'],W['W_k.0'],W['W_v.0']])*D)]:
    sv=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)
    print(name,'stack sv',sv[::12].round(2))

```

## Call 6

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
D=W['ln1_w.0']
h=lambda n,j: W[n][64*j:64*j+64]
def rs(A,k):
    u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return vt[:k],s
# subspaces of V in heads 1,5
def angles(A,B):
    return np.linalg.svd(A@B.T,compute_uv=False)
V1,s1=rs(h('W_v.0',1),30); V5,s5=rs(h('W_v.0',5),30)
print('V1 sv',s1[:36].round(2)); print('V5 sv',s5[:36].round(2))
for k in [10,20,24,26,28,30]:
    print(k,'cos V1/V5',angles(V1[:k],V5[:k]).round(2)[[0,k//2,k-3,k-2,k-1]])
# random baseline
r=np.linalg.qr(np.random.randn(384,28))[0].T
print('rand cos',angles(r,V5[:28]).round(2)[:3])
# Joint token subspace: SVD of stacked V1,V5
S=np.vstack([h('W_v.0',1),h('W_v.0',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False)
print('stacked V1,V5 sv',s[:44].round(2))
T=vt[:28]
for j in range(6):
    for n in ['W_q.0','W_k.0','W_v.0']:
        A=h(n,j)
        f=np.linalg.norm(A@T.T)**2/np.linalg.norm(A)**2
        # top-3 singular vectors share in T
        uu,ss,vv=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)
        print(j,n,'frac energy in T %.3f'%f,'top sv in T:',(np.linalg.norm(vv[:6]@T.T,axis=1)**2).round(2))

import numpy as np

for l in range(4):
    D=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    print('=== LAYER',l)
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][s]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][s]; V=W[f'W_v.{l}'][s]; O=W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,s]
        QD=Q*D;KD=K*D
        sq=np.linalg.svd(QD,compute_uv=False); sk=np.linalg.svd(KD,compute_uv=False)
        B=(QD.T@KD)/8
        sb=np.linalg.svd(B,compute_uv=False)
        A=O@(V*D); sa=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)
        ev=np.linalg.eigvals(B); 
        print(f'H{j} QD {sq[[0,1,2,5,10,20,30,40,50]].round(2)} KD {sk[[0,1,2,5,10,20,30,40,50]].round(2)}')
        print(f'    B sv {sb[[0,1,2,3,5,10,20,30,40]].round(2)} fro {np.linalg.norm(B):.2f} sym {np.linalg.norm((B+B.T)/2)/np.linalg.norm(B):.3f} tr {np.trace(B):.2f}; bval(Qb,Kb) |Qb|={np.linalg.norm(Q@W[f"ln1_b.{l}"]):.2f} |Kb|={np.linalg.norm(K@W[f"ln1_b.{l}"]):.2f}')
        print(f'    OVD sv {sa[[0,1,2,3,5,10,20,30,40]].round(2)}')

## Call 8

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=3,suppress=True)
D=W['ln1_w.0']
hd=lambda n,j: W[n][64*j:64*j+64]
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v.0',1),hd('W_v.0',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); T=vt[:28]
Pc=np.eye(384)-T.T@T
for j in [1,5,0,2,3]:
    Q=hd('W_q.0',j)*1.0;K=hd('W_k.0',j)*1.0
    # B in h-coordinates: score(i,j) = h_i^T Q^T K h_j/8
    B=Q.T@K/8
    Bp=Pc@B@Pc
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(Bp)
    o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))[:36]
    print('Head',j,'B fro',np.linalg.norm(B).round(2),'Bp fro',np.linalg.norm(Bp).round(2),'BT fro',np.linalg.norm(T@B@T.T).round(2),'tr B',np.trace(B).round(2),'tr Bp',np.trace(Bp).round(2),'trBT',np.trace(T@B@T.T).round(2))
    print(' |ev|',np.abs(ev[o]).round(2))
    print(' ang deg',np.degrees(np.angle(ev[o])).round(0))

```

## Call 9

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j: W[n][64*j:64*j+64]
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v.0',1),hd('W_v.0',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); T=vt[:28]
Pc=np.eye(384)-T.T@T
B={j:Pc@(hd('W_q.0',j).T@hd('W_k.0',j)/8)@Pc for j in [1,5]}
print('commutator rel', np.linalg.norm(B[1]@B[5]-B[5]@B[1])/(np.linalg.norm(B[1])*np.linalg.norm(B[5])))
R=np.random.randn(384,384)*0
# random baseline: rotate B5 by random orthogonal on P
for a,b in [(1,5),(5,1)]:
    ev,VR=np.linalg.eig(B[a]); 
    VL=np.linalg.pinv(VR)
    o=[i for i in np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)) if ev[i].imag>=0][:16]
    print('eigs of B',a,'projected on B',b)
    for i in o:
        v=VR[:,i]; w=VL[i,:]
        # plain Rayleigh with unit v
        r=(v.conj()@B[b]@v)/(v.conj()@v)
        r2=(w@B[b]@v)
        print(f'  ev {abs(ev[i]):.2f}@{np.degrees(np.angle(ev[i])):6.1f}  in B{b}: rayleigh {abs(r):.2f}@{np.degrees(np.angle(r)):6.1f}  biorth {abs(r2):.2f}@{np.degrees(np.angle(r2)):6.1f}')

```

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]      # h0 coords
Tx=np.linalg.qr((Th/D0).T)[0]                                 # x-space token basis (384x28)
Ox1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T; Ox5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T        # x-space prev channels (384x28), rows indexed by T basis
print('sv Ox5',np.linalg.svd(Ox5,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('sv Ox1',np.linalg.svd(Ox1,compute_uv=False).round(2))
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
def rho(C):
    return np.trace(C)/(np.linalg.norm(C)*np.sqrt(C.shape[0]))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    print('layer',l)
    for j in range(6):
        Q=hd('W_q',j,l)*Dl; K=hd('W_k',j,l)*Dl; V=hd('W_v',j,l)*Dl
        qT=Q@(Th/D0).T   # query read of token, indexed by Th basis 64x28
        kP5=K@Ox5; kP1=K@Ox1; kT=K@(Th/D0).T
        C55=qT.T@kP5; C51=qT.T@kP1; Cself=qT.T@kT
        # baseline: random rotation of the right basis
        R=np.linalg.qr(rng.normal(size=(28,28)))[0]
        print(f' H{j}  rho q(T)->k(prev5) {rho(C55):+.2f} |C|={np.linalg.norm(C55):.1f}  q(T)->k(prev1) {rho(C51):+.2f} |C|={np.linalg.norm(C51):.1f}  q(T)->k(T) {rho(Cself):+.2f} |C|={np.linalg.norm(Cself):.1f}  v(T) {np.linalg.norm(V@(Th/D0).T):.1f} q(T) {np.linalg.norm(qT):.1f} k(T) {np.linalg.norm(kT):.1f}; k(p5) {np.linalg.norm(kP5):.1f} k(p1) {np.linalg.norm(kP1):.1f}; q(p5) {np.linalg.norm(Q@Ox5):.1f} v(p5) {np.linalg.norm(V@Ox5):.1f}')

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
# P subspace in h coords from Q,K of heads 1,5 minus T
QK=np.vstack([hd('W_q',1),hd('W_k',1),hd('W_q',5),hd('W_k',5)])
Pc=np.eye(384)-Th.T@Th
uq,sq,vq=np.linalg.svd(QK@Pc,full_matrices=False)
print('QK(1,5) minus T sv',sq[:90:3].round(2))
Ph=vq[:60]
for j in [4,0,2,3]:
    A=oh(j)@(hd('W_v',j))
    U,Sg,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)
    print('Head',j,'OV sv',Sg[:6].round(2))
    for r in range(3):
        v=Vt[r]; uu=U[:,r]
        top=np.argsort(-np.abs(v))[:8]; topu=np.argsort(-np.abs(uu))[:8]
        print(f'  r{r}: inT {np.linalg.norm(Th@v)**2:.2f} inP {np.linalg.norm(Ph@v)**2:.2f} cos(v,b0) {v@W["ln1_b.0"]/np.linalg.norm(W["ln1_b.0"]):+.2f} cos(v,D0) {v@D0/np.linalg.norm(D0):+.2f}')
        print('     read coords',top,v[top].round(2),' write coords',topu,uu[topu].round(2))
# alignment among heads' top dirs
Us={};Vs={}
for j in range(6):
    A=oh(j)@(hd('W_v',j))
    U,Sg,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); Us[j]=U[:,:2];Vs[j]=Vt[:2]
for a in [4,0,2,3]:
    for b in [4,0,2,3]:
        if a<b: print(a,b,'write cos',np.linalg.svd(Us[a].T@Us[b],compute_uv=False).round(2),'read cos',np.linalg.svd(Vs[a]@Vs[b].T,compute_uv=False).round(2))

## Call 12

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)                                 # 28x384 x-space basis (non-orthonormal)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*D2)@Tb.T                    # 1536x28
print('R sv',np.linalg.svd(R,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('row norms quantiles',np.quantile(np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1),[.1,.5,.9,.99]).round(2))
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(Y,axis=0)
print('kurt',np.sort(k)[::-1].round(1))
# skew: sources sign
from scipy.stats import skew
print('skew',skew(Y,axis=0).round(1))
# how many neurons strongly selective per source
print('frac |Y|>3',(np.abs(Y)>3).mean(0).round(3))
np.save('/tmp/Y0.npy',Y); np.save('/tmp/unmix0.npy',ica.components_)

```

## Call 13

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*D2)@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1)
print('hist log rn',np.histogram(np.log10(rn),bins=12))
# baseline: random dirs in h-space
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
Rb=rng.normal(size=(28,384)); Rb=np.linalg.qr(Rb.T)[0].T/D0
R0=(W['W_fc.0']*D2)@Rb.T
print('baseline sv',np.linalg.svd(R0,compute_uv=False)[:6].round(2))
act=rn>4
print('active neurons',act.sum())
Ra=R[act]
print('active sv',np.linalg.svd(Ra,compute_uv=False).round(1))
# neurons' read of prev channels and of P
F=W['W_fc.0']*D2
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T
r5=np.linalg.norm(F@P5,axis=1); r1=np.linalg.norm(F@P1,axis=1)
print('active: T read med',np.median(rn[act]).round(1),' p5 read',np.median(r5[act]).round(1),' p1 read',np.median(r1[act]).round(1))
print('inactive: T read med',np.median(rn[~act]).round(1),' p5 read',np.median(r5[~act]).round(1),' p1 read',np.median(r1[~act]).round(1))
for seed in range(3):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=27,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed,fun='logcosh')
    Y=ica.fit_transform(Ra)
    print('seed',seed,'kurt',np.sort(kurtosis(Y,axis=0))[::-1].round(1)[:27])
    print('   skew',np.sort(skew(Y,axis=0)).round(1))

```

## Call 15

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
Ud=U[:,1:28]   # 27 dense output dims
C=(Ud.T@P).T   # 1536x27 columns in U coords
cn=np.linalg.norm(C,axis=1)
print('col norm quant',np.quantile(cn,[.1,.25,.5,.75,.9,.99]).round(2))
print('hist',np.histogram(np.log10(cn),bins=12)[0])
act=cn>np.quantile(cn,.5)
for seed in [0,1]:
    ica=FastICA(n_components=27,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed,fun='logcosh')
    Y=ica.fit_transform(C[act])
    print('kurt',np.sort(kurtosis(Y,axis=0))[::-1].round(1))
    print('skew',np.sort(skew(Y,axis=0)).round(1))
# also ICA on W_o.3 columns in U coords
Co=(Ud.T@W['W_o.3']).T
print('W_o.3 frac energy in Ud per head',[round(float(np.linalg.norm(Ud.T@oh(j,3))**2/np.linalg.norm(oh(j,3))**2),3) for j in range(6)])
print('Random 27-dim frac would be',27/384)
print('W_o.2 frac energy in Ud',[round(float(np.linalg.norm(Ud.T@oh(j,2))**2/np.linalg.norm(oh(j,2))**2),3) for j in range(6)])

```

## Call 16

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*D2)@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act])
tau=ica.components_.copy()            # 28x28, tau[c] token coordinate in Th basis
print('kurt',kurtosis(Y,axis=0).round(1))
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
# strength measures
Vs=[hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)]
print('active neurons with Y>3 per token',(Y>3).sum(0)); print('Y<-3',(Y<-3).sum(0))
print('|V1 tau|',np.linalg.norm(hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T,axis=0).round(2))
print('|V5 tau|',np.linalg.norm(hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T,axis=0).round(2))
# token->output maps through L3 copy heads
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
Mh={}
for j in [1,5,0,2,3,4]:
    Mh[j]=Ud.T@oh(j,3)@(hd('W_v',j,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T   # 27x28
    print('H',j,'norm per token',np.linalg.norm(Mh[j],axis=0).round(1))
c=lambda A,B:(A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)).T@(B/np.linalg.norm(B,axis=0))
print('diag cos M1 vs M5',np.diag(c(Mh[1],Mh[5])).round(2))
X=c(Mh[1],Mh[5]); off=X[~np.eye(28,dtype=bool)]; print('offdiag mean/std',off.mean().round(3),off.std().round(3))
print('M1 svd',np.linalg.svd(Mh[1],compute_uv=False)[[0,5,10,20,26]].round(1),'M5 svd',np.linalg.svd(Mh[5],compute_uv=False)[[0,5,10,20,26]].round(1))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*D2)@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(M1)+n(M5)
Wout=np.linalg.pinv(O)            # 28x27 logit functional in Ud coords
print('sv O',np.linalg.svd(O,compute_uv=False).round(2))
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T)                   # 384x28 x-space token dirs (unnormalised scale)
d=Ud.T@tx
print('frac of token dir in Ud',(np.linalg.norm(d,axis=0)**2/np.linalg.norm(tx,axis=0)**2).round(3))
A=Wout@d                          # A[c,c'] logit c given token c' (scale arbitrary per c')
A=A/np.std(A,axis=0,keepdims=True)
A=A-A.mean(1,keepdims=True)
print('A top sv',np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False).round(1))
for c in range(28):
    o=np.argsort(-A[:,c])
    print(c,'->',[int(x) for x in o[:6]],' | low',[int(x) for x in o[-4:]],' vals',A[o[:3],c].round(1))

```

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'])@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(M1)+n(M5)
Wout=np.linalg.pinv(O)
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T)
Ls={}
for k in range(4):
    L=Wout@Ud.T@W[f'W_proj.{k}']@(W[f'W_fc.{k}']*W[f'ln2_w.{k}'])@tx
    Ls[k]=L
    c=L-L.mean(0,keepdims=True); c=c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    print('L',k,'sv',np.linalg.svd(c,compute_uv=False)[:8].round(1))
L=Ls[3]; c=L-L.mean(1,keepdims=True); c=c/np.std(c,axis=0,keepdims=True)
print('correlation of A-type structure: list for L3 path')
for t in range(28):
    o=np.argsort(-c[:,t])
    print(t,'->',[int(x) for x in o[:7]],' | low',[int(x) for x in o[-4:]],' vals',c[o[:3],t].round(1))

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'])@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
th=tau@Th   # h0-coords token dirs, 28x384
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T).T # x-coords 28x384
print('tok x norm',np.linalg.norm(tx,axis=1).round(1))
print('Token   ',np.arange(28))
for l in range(4):
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    base=tx if l>0 else tx
    for name in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        print(f'L{l} {name}')
        for j in range(6):
            A=(hd(name,j,l)*Dl)@base.T   # 64x28
            print('  H',j,np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0).round(1))

## Call 20

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, leaves_list, fcluster
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
R=(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'])@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
# neuron selectivity
print('n active',Y.shape[0])
order=np.argsort(-np.abs(Y).max(1))
print('example neurons (top tokens with value):')
for n in order[:30]:
    o=np.argsort(-np.abs(Y[n]))[:5]
    print(' ',[(int(i),round(float(Y[n,i]),1)) for i in o])
print('n tokens with |Y|>1.5 per neuron hist',np.bincount((np.abs(Y)>1.5).sum(1)))
# correlation among token columns
C=np.corrcoef(Y.T)
L=linkage(Y.T,'average',metric='correlation')
print('leaf order',leaves_list(L))
print('top corr pairs')
iu=np.triu_indices(28,1); idx=np.argsort(-C[iu])[:15]
print([(int(iu[0][i]),int(iu[1][i]),round(float(C[iu][i]),2)) for i in idx])
idx=np.argsort(C[iu])[:10]
print([(int(iu[0][i]),int(iu[1][i]),round(float(C[iu][i]),2)) for i in idx])

```

## Call 21

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Rc=R[act]-R[act].mean(0)
Ys=Rc@tau.T
# scale check
print('Y vs Ys corr',np.corrcoef(Y[:,0],Ys[:,0])[0,1])
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T ; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y1=Fa@P1
print('norm cur',np.linalg.norm(Ys),'prev5',np.linalg.norm(Y5),'prev1',np.linalg.norm(Y1))
print('per-token norm Y5',np.linalg.norm(Y5,axis=0).round(1))
print('per-token norm Y1',np.linalg.norm(Y1,axis=0).round(1))
sc=np.std(Y5); 
for c in range(28):
    ns=np.argsort(-Y[:,c])[:4]
    print('tok',c)
    for n in ns:
        if Y[n,c]<4: continue
        p=np.argsort(-Y5[n]); q=np.argsort(Y5[n])
        p1=np.argsort(-Y1[n])
        print(f'   n{n} Y={Y[n,c]:.1f} prev+ {[(int(i),round(float(Y5[n,i]/sc),1)) for i in p[:4]]} prev- {[(int(i),round(float(Y5[n,i]/sc),1)) for i in q[:2]]} lag2+ {[int(i) for i in p1[:3]]}')

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(M1)+n(M5); Wout=np.linalg.pinv(O)
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T)
mats={'direct':Wout@Ud.T@tx}
for k in range(4):
    mats[f'mlp{k}']=Wout@Ud.T@W[f'W_proj.{k}']@(W[f'W_fc.{k}']*W[f'ln2_w.{k}'])@tx
def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); return M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)
U0={}
for k,M in mats.items():
    u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(dc(M))
    if u[1,0]<0: u=-u; vt=-vt
    U0[k]=(u,s,vt)
    print(k,'sv',s[:6].round(1))
    print('  out-load',(u[:,0]*10).round(0).astype(int))
    print('  in-load ',(vt[0]*10).round(0).astype(int))
ks=list(mats)
print('corr of double-centered matrices')
print(np.corrcoef([dc(mats[k]).ravel() for k in ks]).round(2))
# remove top mode and correlate residual
res=[]
for k in ks:
    u,s,vt=U0[k]; res.append((dc(mats[k])-s[0]*np.outer(u[:,0],vt[0])).ravel())
print('residual corr');print(np.corrcoef(res).round(2))

```

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(M1)+n(M5)
print('sv O',np.linalg.svd(O,compute_uv=False).round(2))
lam=1.0
Wout=O.T@np.linalg.inv(O@O.T+lam*np.eye(27))
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T)
mats={'direct':Wout@Ud.T@tx}
for k in range(4):
    mats[f'mlp{k}']=Wout@Ud.T@W[f'W_proj.{k}']@(W[f'W_fc.{k}']*W[f'ln2_w.{k}'])@tx
def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); return M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)
for k,M in mats.items():
    u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(dc(M))
    print(k,'sv',s[:8].round(1))
ks=list(mats)
print(np.corrcoef([dc(mats[k]).ravel() for k in ks]).round(2))
A=dc(mats['direct']); A=A/A.std()
L=dc(mats['mlp3']); L=L/L.std()
G=dc(mats['mlp0']); G=G/G.std()
Z=(A+L+G)/3
for t in range(28):
    o=np.argsort(-Z[:,t])
    print(t,'->',[int(x) for x in o[:7]],' | low',[int(x) for x in o[-4:]],' vals',Z[o[:3],t].round(1))

import numpy as np, warnings, re, random
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)

for k in range(4):

def dc(M):

py="""import os
import sys
from typing import list
class model(object):
    def __init__(self, name, value=none):
        self.name = name
        self.value = value
        self._data = {}
    def get(self, key, default=none):
        if key in self._data:
            return self._data[key]
        return default
    def set(self, key, value):
        self._data[key] = value

VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def pmi(t,a=0.5):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    Pj=C/C.sum(); r=Pj.sum(1,keepdims=True); c=Pj.sum(0,keepdims=True)
    M=np.log(Pj/(r@c)); M=np.clip(M,-3,3); return dc(M)
refs={'english':pmi(eng),'python':pmi(py),'romaji':pmi(ro)}
print({k:len(norm(v)) for k,v in [('e',eng),('p',py),('r',ro)]})
def qap(Z,Ref,restarts=300,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None)
    N=28
    for r in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(N)
        cur=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum()
        imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    # delta only rows/cols a,b
                    Rq=Ref[np.ix_(q,q)]
                    v=(Z*Rq).sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9:
                        p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best

        print(name,k,'r=%.3f'%r,''.join(VOC[i] if i>0 else '$' for i in p).replace(' ','_'),'t',round(time.time()-t))

import numpy as np, warnings, re, time
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5c=Y5-Y5.mean(0)
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
M1=Ud.T@oh(1,3)@(hd('W_v',1,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
M5=Ud.T@oh(5,3)@(hd('W_v',5,3)*D3)@Tb.T@tau.T
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(M1)+n(M5)
Wout=O.T@np.linalg.inv(O@O.T+1.0*np.eye(27))
tx=(Tb.T@tau.T)
mats={'direct':Wout@Ud.T@tx}
for k in [0,3]:
    mats[f'mlp{k}']=Wout@Ud.T@W[f'W_proj.{k}']@(W[f'W_fc.{k}']*W[f'ln2_w.{k}'])@tx
def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); return M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)
Zs={k:(dc(v)/dc(v).std()).T for k,v in mats.items()}
SS=Y5c.T@Y; SS=SS-np.diag(np.diag(SS)); Zs['S5']=dc(SS)/dc(SS).std()
eng="""the history of the city began in the early nineteenth century when settlers from the eastern states moved into the region and established a small trading post on the river
it was incorporated as a town in and became the county seat a few years later the population grew rapidly after the arrival of the railroad which connected the area with major markets
during the second world war the city served as an important centre for the production of aircraft and munitions and many of its residents worked in the factories
the university was founded by a group of local citizens who wanted to provide higher education for the people of the state it has since grown into one of the largest institutions in the country with more than thirty thousand students
he was born in london and educated at the royal academy of music where he studied the piano and composition he later moved to paris where he worked as a conductor and published several works including an opera and two symphonies
the species is found in tropical forests of south america where it feeds mainly on insects and small fruit adults are about five centimetres long and have a dark brown body with white spots on the wings
the album was released in june and reached number three on the billboard chart it received generally positive reviews from critics who praised the production and the vocal performance the band toured europe and japan to support the record
according to the census there were people households and families residing in the village the population density was inhabitants per square mile the racial makeup of the village was white african american and other races
the government announced a new policy that would change the way taxes are collected from small businesses and the minister said that the reforms were necessary because the economy had been weakened by the crisis
the river flows through a narrow valley before joining the main stream near the northern border of the province the quality of the water has improved since the construction of a modern treatment plant
in mathematics a group is a set equipped with an operation that combines any two elements to form a third element quantum theory requires that the equations be written in a complex space and the exact value of the function can be found by an integral
"""
ro="""watashi wa sono hito wo tsune ni sensei to yonde ita dakara koko demo tada sensei to kaku dake de honmyou wa uchiakenai
kore wa seken wo habakaru enryo to iu yori mo sono hou ga watashi ni totte shizen dakara de aru watashi wa sono hito no kioku wo yobiokosu goto ni sugu sensei to iitaku naru
wagahai wa neko de aru namae wa mada nai doko de umareta ka tonto kentou ga tsukanu nandemo usugurai jimejime shita tokoro de nyaanyaa naite ita koto dake wa kioku shite iru
kuni kyou no nagai tonneru wo nukeru to yukiguni de atta yoru no soko ga shiroku natta shingoujo ni kisha ga tomatta
aru hi no koto de gozaimasu oshakasama wa gokuraku no hasu ike no fuchi wo hitori de burabura oaruki ni natte irasshaimashita
sono toki kare wa futo tachidomatte ushiro wo furikaetta onna wa damatta mama shizuka ni kochira wo mite ita
kare wa nani mo iwazu ni heya wo dete itta sore kara nagai aida kaette konakatta haha wa mainichi mado no soba ni suwatte michi wo nagamete ita
mukashi mukashi aru tokoro ni ojiisan to obaasan ga sunde imashita ojiisan wa yama e shibakari ni obaasan wa kawa e sentaku ni ikimashita
shikashi sore wa kesshite kantan na koto de wa nakatta jibun no kokoro no naka ni aru mono wo hito ni tsutaeru no wa muzukashii kare wa sou omotte chiisaku tameiki wo tsuita
"""
VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def pmi(t,a=0.5):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    Pj=C/C.sum(); r=Pj.sum(1,keepdims=True); c=Pj.sum(0,keepdims=True)
    M=np.log(Pj/(r@c)); M=np.clip(M,-3,3); return dc(M)
refs={'english':pmi(eng),'romaji':pmi(ro)}
def qap(Z,Ref,tlimit,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None); N=28; t0=time.time()
    while time.time()-t0<tlimit:
        p=rng.permutation(N); cur=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    v=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(q,q)]).sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best
rng=np.random.default_rng(5)
for mn,Mz in Zs.items():
    line=mn
    for k,Rf in refs.items():
        sc,p=qap(Mz,Rf,9)
        r=np.corrcoef(Mz.ravel(),Rf[np.ix_(p,p)].ravel())[0,1]
        sc0,p0=qap(Mz[rng.permutation(28)],Rf,9,seed=1)
        line+=f' | {k} r={r:.3f} null {sc0/np.linalg.norm(Mz)/np.linalg.norm(Rf):.3f} ' + ''.join(VOC[i] if i>0 else '$' for i in p).replace(' ','_')
    print(line,flush=True)

import numpy as np, warnings, re, time
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, leaves_list, fcluster
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T ; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5c=Y5-Y5.mean(0); Y1=Fa@P1; Y1c=Y1-Y1.mean(0)
# remove neurons' self-channel leak: regress Y5c columns on Y columns? use all neurons in F (not just act) for prev tuning
Fall=F
Ya5=Fall@P5; Ya5=Ya5-Ya5.mean(0)
print('per token prev-tuning norm over all neurons',np.linalg.norm(Ya5,axis=0).round(1))
for name,X in [('Y5 active',Y5c),('Y5 all',Ya5),('Y1 active',Y1c)]:
    C=np.corrcoef(X.T)
    u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(X-X.mean(0),full_matrices=False)
    print(name,'sv',s[:10].round(1))
    for r in range(4):
        o=np.argsort(vt[r]); print('  PC',r,'neg',[int(i) for i in o[:7]],' pos',[int(i) for i in o[-7:][::-1]])
    L=linkage(X.T,'average',metric='correlation')
    print('  leaves',leaves_list(L))
    print('  clusters k=5',fcluster(L,5,'maxclust'))
    print('  top corr',sorted([(round(float(C[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:14])

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T                      # 384x28 current token
p5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T    # lag1 channel
p1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T    # lag2 channel
def z(C):
    d=np.diag(C); off=C[~np.eye(28,dtype=bool)]
    return (d.mean()-off.mean())/off.std(), (d>off.mean()+off.std()).sum()
print('format: z(q cur, k lag1) nPos | z(q cur,k lag2) | z(q lag1,k lag2) | z(q cur,k cur)  | z(q lag1,k lag1)')
for l in [1,2,3]:
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=hd('W_q',j,l)*Dl; K=hd('W_k',j,l)*Dl
        c1=z((Q@tx).T@(K@p5)); c2=z((Q@tx).T@(K@p1)); c3=z((Q@p5).T@(K@p1)); c4=z((Q@tx).T@(K@tx)); c5=z((Q@p5).T@(K@p5))
        print(f'L{l}H{j}  cur~lag1 {c1[0]:+.2f} ({c1[1]:2d})  cur~lag2 {c2[0]:+.2f} ({c2[1]:2d})  lag1~lag2 {c3[0]:+.2f} ({c3[1]:2d})  cur~cur {c4[0]:+.2f} ({c4[1]:2d})  lag1~lag1 {c5[0]:+.2f} ({c5[1]:2d})')

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Pc=np.eye(384)-Th.T@Th
B5=Pc@(hd('W_q',5).T@hd('W_k',5)/8)@Pc
ev,VR=np.linalg.eig(B5)
o=[i for i in np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)) if ev[i].imag>1e-6][:12]
modes=[(np.degrees(np.angle(ev[i])),abs(ev[i]),VR[:,i]) for i in o]
print('modes omega(deg) from L0H5 assuming lag 1:',[round(m[0],1) for m in modes],'amp',[round(m[1],2) for m in modes])
wg=np.array([m[0] for m in modes])
for l in range(4):
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    print('--- layer',l)
    for j in range(6):
        B=hd('W_q',j,l).T@hd('W_k',j,l)/8
        amps=[];phs=[]
        for w,a,v in modes:
            vv=(Dl/D0)*v
            r=(vv.conj()@B@vv)/(vv.conj()@vv)
            amps.append(abs(r)); phs.append(np.degrees(np.angle(r)))
        amps=np.array(amps);phs=np.array(phs)
        print(f'H{j} amp {amps.round(2)}  phase {phs.round(0)}')

## Call 31

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
Ms={j:Ud.T@oh(j,3)@(hd('W_v',j,3)*D3)@tx for j in range(6)}
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(Ms[1])+n(Ms[5])
# similarity of output vectors between tokens
G=(n(O).T@n(O)); 
print('output dir self-similarity top pairs',sorted([(round(float(G[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:16])
print('most negative',sorted([(round(float(G[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)])[:10])
u_,s_,vt_=np.linalg.svd(O-O.mean(1,keepdims=True),full_matrices=False)
print('O sv',s_.round(2))
for r in range(4):
    o=np.argsort(vt_[r]); print('O PC',r,'neg',[int(i) for i in o[:8]],'pos',[int(i) for i in o[-8:][::-1]])
# head maps vs copy map diag cos
for j in [0,2,3,4]:
    X=n(Ms[j]).T@n(O); print('L3 head',j,'diag cos with copy map',np.diag(X).round(2),' mean',np.diag(X).mean().round(2),' off mean',X[~np.eye(28,dtype=bool)].mean().round(3))
for j in [1,5]:
    other=5 if j==1 else 1
    X=n(Ms[j]).T@n(Ms[other]); print('head',j,'vs',other,'diag mean',np.diag(X).mean().round(2))
# energy of T in V for L3 heads and fraction of OV into Ud
for j in range(6):
    V=hd('W_v',j,3)*D3; O3=oh(j,3)
    a=np.linalg.norm(V@tx/np.linalg.norm(tx,axis=0))
    A=O3@V
    print('L3 H',j,'V on tok',round(float(a),1),'|V|',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V)),1),' OV into Ud frac',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Ud.T@A)**2/np.linalg.norm(A)**2),3))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']; D3=W['ln1_w.3']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=np.where(rn>4)[0]
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
P3=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P3,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
Ms={j:Ud.T@oh(j,3)@(hd('W_v',j,3)*D3)@tx for j in [1,5]}
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(Ms[1])+n(Ms[5])
for lam in [1.0]:
    Wout=O.T@np.linalg.inv(O@O.T+lam*np.eye(27))
    print('out fraction of W_proj.0 energy in Ud',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Ud.T@W['W_proj.0'])**2/np.linalg.norm(W['W_proj.0'])**2),3))
    # L0 detector neurons' direct output votes
    V0=Wout@Ud.T@W['W_proj.0'][:,act]    # 28 x n_act
    # L3: same for neurons of L3 that read current token
    F3=W['W_fc.3']*W['ln2_w.3']; R3=F3@tx
    print('L3 neurons reading token: row-norm quantiles',np.quantile(np.linalg.norm(R3,axis=1),[.1,.5,.9,.99]).round(1))
    V3=Wout@Ud.T@W['W_proj.3']          # 28 x 1536
    Tn=R3/np.linalg.norm(R3,axis=1,keepdims=True)
    for c in range(28):
        ns=np.argsort(-Y[:,c])[:3]
        v=V0[:,ns]*Y[ns,c]; v=v.sum(1); v=(v-v.mean())/v.std()
        o=np.argsort(-v)[:5]
        # L3 neurons best tuned to token c
        ns3=np.argsort(-R3[:,c])[:12]
        w=(V3[:,ns3]*R3[ns3,c]).sum(1); w=(w-w.mean())/w.std(); o3=np.argsort(-w)[:5]
        print(f'tok {c:2d} L0det-> {[int(i) for i in o]} z {v[o[:2]].round(1)}   L3tuned-> {[int(i) for i in o3]} z {w[o3[:2]].round(1)}  low {[int(i) for i in np.argsort(w)[:3]]}')

## Call 33

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
print('Tok idx        ',np.arange(28))
for l in range(4):
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}'];b=W[f'ln1_b.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=hd('W_q',j,l);K=hd('W_k',j,l)
        q0=Q@b
        g=(q0@(K*Dl)@tx)/8
        g=g-g.mean()
        k0=K@b
        g2=((Q*Dl)@tx).T@k0/8; g2=g2-g2.mean()
        print(f'L{l}H{j} key-pref',g.round(1)); 
        print(f'     cur-pref',g2.round(1))

## Call 34

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
th=tau@Th   # token dirs in h0 coords (28x384)
for j in [0,1,2,3,5]:
    q=hd('W_q',j)@th.T; k=hd('W_k',j)@th.T; v=hd('W_v',j)@th.T
    C=q.T@k/8
    C=C-C.mean(0,keepdims=True); C=C-C.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    u_,s_,vt_=np.linalg.svd(C)
    print(f'L0H{j} B(token,token) sv',s_[:6].round(2))
    for r in range(3):
        a=u_[:,r]; b=vt_[r]
        if np.abs(a).max()!=a.max(): a=-a;b=-b
        oa=np.argsort(-a); ob=np.argsort(-b)
        print(f'  mode{r}: query+ {[ (int(i),round(float(a[i]),2)) for i in oa[:5]]} query- {[ (int(i),round(float(a[i]),2)) for i in oa[-3:]]}')
        print(f'         key+   {[ (int(i),round(float(b[i]),2)) for i in ob[:5]]} key- {[ (int(i),round(float(b[i]),2)) for i in ob[-3:]]}')
    vn=np.linalg.norm(v,axis=0)
    print('  V token read order',[int(i) for i in np.argsort(-vn)[:10]],vn[np.argsort(-vn)[:10]].round(2))

```

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T ; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y1=Fa@P1; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); Y1=Y1-Y1.mean(0)
z5=Y5/Y5.std(); z1=Y1/Y1.std()
print('tok: best double (min(cur,prev)) ; lag2 same-token at that neuron ; best triple')
for c in range(28):
    d=np.minimum(Y[:,c],z5[:,c]); n=np.argmax(d)
    t=np.minimum(np.minimum(Y[:,c],z5[:,c]),z1[:,c]); m=np.argmax(t)
    # self channels regression across detectors: slope of z5[c] and z1[c] on Y[c]
    x=Y[:,c]; sel=x>3
    print(f'{c:2d} double n{n} cur {Y[n,c]:.1f} prev {z5[n,c]:.1f} lag2 {z1[n,c]:.1f} | triple n{m} cur {Y[m,c]:.1f} prev {z5[m,c]:.1f} lag2 {z1[m,c]:.1f}  | detectors n={sel.sum()} mean prev-self {z5[sel,c].mean():.1f} mean lag2-self {z1[sel,c].mean():.1f}')

aru hi no koto de gozaimasu oshakasama wa gokuraku no hasu ike no fuchi wo hitori de burabura oaruki ni natte irasshaimashita
sono toki kare wa futo tachidomatte ushiro wo furikaetta onna wa damatta mama shizuka ni kochira wo mite ita
kare wa nani mo iwazu ni heya wo dete itta sore kara nagai aida kaette konakatta haha wa mainichi mado no soba ni suwatte michi wo nagamete ita

shikashi sore wa kesshite kantan na koto de wa nakatta jibun no kokoro no naka ni aru mono wo hito ni tsutaeru no wa muzukashii kare wa sou omotte chiisaku tameiki wo tsuita
gakkou no kaeri ni tomodachi to issho ni kawa no hotori wo aruite iru to tooku kara kane no oto ga kikoete kita sora wa akaku some hajimete ita
nihon no bungaku ni okeru shizenkan wa seiyou no sore to wa hijou ni kotonatte iru to iwarete iru shikashi kono mondai ni tsuite wa sara ni kuwashiku kangaete minakereba naranai
ano otoko wa dare da to kiku to chichi wa shiranai to kotaeta ga sono kao wa aoku natte ita
"""
VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def sims(t,a=0.3):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    Pn=C/C.sum(1,keepdims=True); Pp=(C/C.sum(0,keepdims=True)).T
    s1=np.corrcoef(np.sqrt(Pn)); s2=np.corrcoef(np.sqrt(Pp))
    np.fill_diagonal(s1,0); np.fill_diagonal(s2,0)
    return s1,s2
refs={k:sims(v) for k,v in [('english',eng),('python',py),('romaji',ro)]}
def qap(Z1,Z2,R1,R2,tlimit,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None); N=28; t0=time.time()
    f=lambda p:(Z1*R1[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum()/np.linalg.norm(R1)+(Z2*R2[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum()/np.linalg.norm(R2)
    while time.time()-t0<tlimit:
        p=rng.permutation(N); cur=f(p); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    v=f(q)
                    if v>cur+1e-9: p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
A=Csucc/np.linalg.norm(Csucc); B=Cpred/np.linalg.norm(Cpred)
for k,(r1,r2) in refs.items():
    sc,p=qap(A,B,r1,r2,30)
    r_1=np.corrcoef(A.ravel(),r1[np.ix_(p,p)].ravel())[0,1]; r_2=np.corrcoef(B.ravel(),r2[np.ix_(p,p)].ravel())[0,1]
    pp=rng.permutation(28)
    sc0,p0=qap(A,B[np.ix_(pp,pp)],r1,r2,30,seed=2)
    print(k,'score %.3f null %.3f'%(sc,sc0),'r succ %.3f r pred %.3f'%(r_1,r_2),''.join(VOC[i] if i>0 else '$' for i in p).replace(' ','_'),flush=True)

rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
p5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T ; p1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
print('tok          ',np.arange(28))
for l,j in [(2,1),(3,1),(3,5),(3,0),(2,2)]:
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']; Q=hd('W_q',j,l)*Dl; K=hd('W_k',j,l)*Dl
    C1=(Q@tx).T@(K@p5)/8; C2=(Q@p5).T@(K@p1)/8; C3=(Q@p5).T@(K@p5)/8
    for nm,C in [('cur~lag1',C1),('lag1~lag2',C2),('lag1~lag1',C3)]:
        off=C[~np.eye(28,dtype=bool)]
        d=(np.diag(C)-off.mean())/off.std()
        print(f'L{l}H{j} {nm:10s}',d.round(1))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0)
SS=Y5.T@Y; SS0=SS.copy(); SS=SS-np.diag(np.diag(SS))
u_,s_,vt_=np.linalg.svd(SS-SS.mean(0,keepdims=True)-SS.mean(1,keepdims=True)+SS.mean())
print('sv',s_[:8].round(0))
for r in range(4):
    print('mode',r)
    a=u_[:,r];b=vt_[r]
    print(' prev-load',(a*10).round(0).astype(int))
    print(' cur-load ',(b*10).round(0).astype(int))
    print(' corr',np.corrcoef(a,b)[0,1].round(2))
print('diag',np.diag(SS0).round(0))
# class via mode0
a=u_[:,0];b=vt_[0]
print('sorted by prev-load:',[(int(i),round(float(a[i]),2),round(float(b[i]),2)) for i in np.argsort(a)])

```

## Call 39

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, leaves_list, fcluster, dendrogram
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
def sim(maps):
    X=np.vstack(maps)         # features x 28
    X=X-X.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    X=X/np.linalg.norm(X,axis=0,keepdims=True)
    return X.T@X
mapsets={}
for l in [1,2,3]:
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']; D2=W[f'ln2_w.{l}']
    mapsets[f'L{l}qkv']=[(W[f'W_q.{l}']*Dl)@tx,(W[f'W_k.{l}']*Dl)@tx,(W[f'W_v.{l}']*Dl)@tx]
    mapsets[f'L{l}fc']=[(W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2)@tx]
sims={k:sim(v) for k,v in mapsets.items()}
Sall=sum(sims.values())/len(sims)
# agreement between maps
ks=list(sims)
iu=np.triu_indices(28,1)
print('corr of similarity matrices');print(np.corrcoef([sims[k][iu] for k in ks]).round(2))
print('Mean similarity: neighbours')
for c in range(28):
    o=np.argsort(-Sall[c]); o=[i for i in o if i!=c][:5]
    print(c,[(int(i),round(float(Sall[c,i]),2)) for i in o])
L=linkage(Sall[iu]*0+0,'average') if False else None
from scipy.spatial.distance import squareform
D=1-Sall; np.fill_diagonal(D,0); D=(D+D.T)/2
L=linkage(squareform(D,checks=False),'average')
print('leaves',leaves_list(L))
for k in [3,5,8,12]:
    print(k,fcluster(L,k,'maxclust'))

```

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T ; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y1=Fa@P1; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); Y1=Y1-Y1.mean(0)
z5=Y5/Y5.std(); z1=Y1/Y1.std()
th_c=3.0; th_p=2.5
pairs=np.zeros((28,28),int)
for n in range(Y.shape[0]):
    cs=np.where(Y[n]>th_c)[0]; ps=np.where(z5[n]>th_p)[0]
    for c in cs:
        for p in ps: pairs[p,c]+=1
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250)
print('prev\\cur  ',' '.join(f'{i:2d}' for i in range(28)))
for p in range(28):
    print(f'{p:2d}        ',' '.join(f'{v:2d}' if v else ' .' for v in pairs[p]),'  sum',pairs[p].sum(),' distinct',(pairs[p]>0).sum())
print('cur sum',pairs.sum(0)); print('cur distinct',(pairs>0).sum(0))

import numpy as np, warnings, re, time
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); z5=Y5/Y5.std()
# soft conj score, no thresholds on outputs of neurons: product of positive tuning parts of weights
Cs=(np.clip(z5,0,None).T@np.clip(Y,0,None))
Cs=Cs/np.sqrt((Cs**2).mean())

shikashi sore wa kesshite kantan na koto de wa nakatta jibun no kokoro no naka ni aru mono wo hito ni tsutaeru no wa muzukashii kare wa sou omotte chiisaku tameiki wo tsuita

nihon no bungaku ni okeru shizenkan wa seiyou no sore to wa hijou ni kotonatte iru to iwarete iru shikashi kono mondai ni tsuite wa sara ni kuwashiku kangaete minakereba naranai
ano otoko wa dare da to kiku to chichi wa shiranai to kotaeta ga sono kao wa aoku natte ita
"""
VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def bi(t,a=0.2):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    return np.sqrt(C/C.sum())
refs={'english':bi(eng),'python':bi(py),'romaji':bi(ro)}
def z(M):
    return (M-M.mean())/M.std()
def qap(Z,Ref,tlimit,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None); N=28; t0=time.time()
    while time.time()-t0<tlimit:
        p=rng.permutation(N); cur=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    v=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(q,q)]).sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best
Zc=z(Cs); rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
for k,Rf in refs.items():
    Rz=z(Rf)
    sc,p=qap(Zc,Rz,25); 
    nulls=[qap(Zc[rng.permutation(28)],Rz,8,seed=i+5)[0] for i in range(3)]
    print(k,'r=%.3f'%(sc/784),'nulls',[round(x/784,3) for x in nulls],''.join(VOC[i] if i>0 else '$' for i in p).replace(' ','_'),flush=True)

from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew

hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]

u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]
P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T; P1=oh(1)@hd('W_v',1)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y1=Fa@P1; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); Y1=Y1-Y1.mean(0)
z5=Y5/Y5.std(); z1=Y1/Y1.std()
# list every neuron with strong cur and strong prev tuning, with lag2 tuning; ranked by joint strength
rows=[]
for n in range(Y.shape[0]):
    c=int(np.argmax(Y[n])); p=int(np.argmax(z5[n])); l=np.argsort(-z1[n])[:3]
    rows.append((min(Y[n,c],z5[n,p]),c,p,[(int(i),round(float(z1[n,i]),1)) for i in l],round(float(Y[n,c]),1),round(float(z5[n,p]),1), [ (int(i),round(float(Y[n,i]),1)) for i in np.argsort(-Y[n])[1:3]], [ (int(i),round(float(z5[n,i]),1)) for i in np.argsort(-z5[n])[1:3]]))
rows.sort(key=lambda r:-r[0])
for r in rows[:90]:
    print(f'lag2{r[3]}  prev {r[2]:2d}({r[5]}) {r[7]}  cur {r[1]:2d}({r[4]}) {r[6]}')

import numpy as np, warnings, re, time
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]; P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); z5=Y5/Y5.std()
# pair strengths: for neurons, cur weight x prev weight (positive parts), excluding self pairs
Cp=(np.clip(z5-1.5,0,None).T@np.clip(Y-1.5,0,None))
np.fill_diagonal(Cp,0)
Wp=Cp/Cp.sum()
freq=np.linalg.norm((F@P5)-(F@P5).mean(0),axis=0)   # token importance proxy
print('n pair entries>0',(Cp>0).sum())

the university was founded by a group of local citizens who wanted to provide higher education for the people of the state it has since grown into one of the largest institutions in the country with more than thirty thousand students
he was born in london and educated at the royal academy of music where he studied the piano and composition he later moved to paris where he worked as a conductor and published several works including an opera and two symphonies
the species is found in tropical forests of south america where it feeds mainly on insects and small fruit adults are about five centimetres long and have a dark brown body with white spots on the wings
the album was released in june and reached number three on the billboard chart it received generally positive reviews from critics who praised the production and the vocal performance the band toured europe and japan to support the record
according to the census there were people households and families residing in the village the population density was inhabitants per square mile the racial makeup of the village was white african american and other races
the government announced a new policy that would change the way taxes are collected from small businesses and the minister said that the reforms were necessary because the economy had been weakened by the crisis
the river flows through a narrow valley before joining the main stream near the northern border of the province the quality of the water has improved since the construction of a modern treatment plant
in mathematics a group is a set equipped with an operation that combines any two elements to form a third element quantum theory requires that the equations be written in a complex space and the exact value of the function can be found by an integral
she played for the national team and scored her first goal against sweden in the final of the tournament after retiring from football she became a coach and later served as president of the club
the company was founded in by two brothers and is headquartered in chicago it manufactures electronic components and employs over five hundred people in its plants across the united states and canada
the church was built in the twelfth century and restored in the nineteenth the tower contains eight bells and the interior has fine stained glass windows and a carved wooden screen
following the death of the king his son succeeded to the throne but the kingdom was soon divided by civil war and the rebels captured the capital after a long siege
"""
py="""import os
import sys
from typing import list
class model(object):
    def __init__(self, name, value=none):
        self.name = name
        self.value = value
        self._data = {}

    def get(self, key, default=none):
        if key in self._data:
            return self._data[key]
        return default

    def set(self, key, value):
        self._data[key] = value

    def to_dict(self):
        result = {}
        for key, value in self._data.items():
            if isinstance(value, list):
                result[key] = [v.to_dict() for v in value]
            else:
                result[key] = value
        return result


def load_config(path):
    with open(path, 'r') as f:
        data = json.load(f)
    return data


def main(args):
    parser = argparse.argumentparser()
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=str, default='out')
    parser.add_argument('--verbose', action='store_true')
    options = parser.parse_args(args)
    if options.verbose:
        logger.info('starting')
    for i in range(len(args)):
        x = np.zeros((n, m))
        y = np.array(x, dtype=np.float32)
        if i == 0:
            continue
        try:
            value = self.get_value(x, y)
        except keyerror as e:
            raise valueerror('invalid key %s' % e)
    return 0


class testfoo(unittest.testcase):
    def setup(self):
        self.client = client()
        self.request = self.factory.get('/')

    def test_get(self):
        response = self.client.get(url)
        self.assertequal(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertequal(len(response.data), 1)

    def test_string(self):
        s = 'hello world'
        self.asserttrue(isinstance(s, str))
        assert result is not none

    @property
    def size(self):
        return self.shape[0] * self.shape[1]

    def forward(self, input, hidden):
        output = self.linear(input)
        output = torch.cat([output, hidden], dim=1)
        return output, hidden

    def get_queryset(self, request):
        qs = super(admin, self).get_queryset(request)
        if not request.user.is_superuser:
            return qs.filter(owner=request.user)
        return qs

    def save(self, commit=true):
        instance = super(form, self).save(commit=false)
        instance.user = self.user
        if commit:
            instance.save()
        return instance

    def process_response(self, request, response):
        if response.status_code != 200:
            return response
        content = response.content.decode('utf-8')
        for name, value in self.headers.items():
            response.headers[name] = value
        return response

    def __repr__(self):
        return '<%s %r>' % (self.__class__.__name__, self.name)

    def read(self, size=-1):
        data = self.stream.read(size)
        if not data:
            self.closed = true
        return data

    def write(self, buffer):
        self.position += len(buffer)
        self.stream.write(buffer)


def compute_loss(labels, logits, weights=none):
    mask = tf.not_equal(labels, 0)
    loss = tf.nn.sparse_softmax_cross_entropy_with_logits(labels=labels, logits=logits)
    return tf.reduce_mean(tf.boolean_mask(loss, mask))

VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def logbi(t,a=0.3):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    L=np.log(C/C.sum())
    return L
refs={'english':logbi(eng),'python':logbi(py),'romaji':logbi(ro)}
print({k:len(norm(v)) for k,v in [('e',eng),('p',py),('r',ro)]})
def qap(Z,Ref,tlimit,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None); N=28; t0=time.time()
    while time.time()-t0<tlimit:
        p=rng.permutation(N); cur=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    v=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(q,q)]).sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best
rng=np.random.default_rng(3)
for k,Rf in refs.items():
    Rz=(Rf-Rf.mean())/Rf.std()
    sc,p=qap(Wp,Rz,70)
    nulls=[qap(Wp[rng.permutation(28)],Rz,20,seed=i+5)[0] for i in range(3)]
    mp={t:(VOC[p[t]] if p[t]>0 else '$') for t in range(28)}
    # most weighted decoded pairs
    idx=np.argsort(-Wp.ravel())[:24]
    dec=[(mp[i//28]+mp[i%28]).replace(' ','_') for i in idx]
    print(k,'score %.3f'%sc,'nulls',[round(x,3) for x in nulls],''.join(mp[t] for t in range(28)).replace(' ','_'),dec,flush=True)

asagohan no shitaku wo shite imasu chichi wa mou kaisha e dekakete shimatta hitobito wa kono machi wo hanarete tooi tokoro e utsutte itta ato ni nokotta no wa toshiyori to chiisana mise bakari de atta sensou ga owatte kara kare wa kuni ni kaette hataraki hajimeta ga sono seikatsu wa kesshite raku de wa nakatta mainichi asa hayaku okite yoru osoku made shigoto wo tsuzuketa

VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r' *',line); lead=m.group(0); rest=line[len(lead):]
        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def logbi(t,a=0.3):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.full((28,28),a)
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    return np.log(C/C.sum())
refs={'english':logbi(eng),'python':logbi(py),'romaji':logbi(ro)}
def qap(Z,Ref,tlimit,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(-1e9,None); N=28; t0=time.time()
    while time.time()-t0<tlimit:
        p=rng.permutation(N); cur=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1,N):
                    q=p.copy(); q[a],q[b]=q[b],q[a]
                    v=(Z*Ref[np.ix_(q,q)]).sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: p=q;cur=v;imp=True
        if cur>best[0]: best=(cur,p.copy())
    return best
N=Y.shape[0]; rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
res={k:[] for k in refs}
for split in range(3):
    perm=rng.permutation(N); A=perm[:N//2]; B=perm[N//2:]
    WA=pairmat(A); WB=pairmat(B)
    for k,Rf in refs.items():
        Rz=(Rf-Rf.mean())/Rf.std()
        for X,Yy in [(WA,WB),(WB,WA)]:
            sc,p=qap(X,Rz,12,seed=split)
            cv=(Yy*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)]).sum()
            res[k].append((round(sc,3),round(cv,3)))
    print(split,{k:res[k][-2:] for k in res},flush=True)
for k in res:
    a=np.array(res[k]); print(k,'fit mean',a[:,0].mean().round(3),'CV mean',a[:,1].mean().round(3),'sd',a[:,1].std().round(3))

from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)

oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]; P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0); z5=Y5/Y5.std()
V=[0,3,7,15,17,25]; Cs=[i for i in range(28) if i not in V]
for th in [1.5,2.5]:
    Cp=(np.clip(z5-th,0,None).T@np.clip(Y-th,0,None)); Dg=np.diag(Cp).copy(); np.fill_diagonal(Cp,0)
    tot=Cp.sum()
    t={}
    t['VtoV']=Cp[np.ix_(V,V)].sum()/tot; t['VtoC']=Cp[np.ix_(V,Cs)].sum()/tot; t['CtoV']=Cp[np.ix_(Cs,V)].sum()/tot; t['CtoC']=Cp[np.ix_(Cs,Cs)].sum()/tot
    print('th',th,{k:round(float(v),3) for k,v in t.items()})
    # expectation if prev partner independent of class given marginal
    rowV=Cp[V].sum()/tot; colV=Cp[:,V].sum()/tot
    print('  marginal prev in V',round(float(rowV),3),' cur in V',round(float(colV),3),' expected CtoC',round(float((1-rowV)*(1-colV)),3))
    # which prev tokens contribute to CtoC most
    rowCC=Cp[np.ix_(Cs,Cs)].sum(1)/Cp[np.ix_(Cs,Cs)].sum()
    print('  CtoC share by prev token',sorted([(round(float(v),3),Cs[i]) for i,v in enumerate(rowCC)],reverse=True)[:10])
    colCC=Cp[np.ix_(Cs,Cs)].sum(0)/Cp[np.ix_(Cs,Cs)].sum()
    print('  CtoC share by cur token',sorted([(round(float(v),3),Cs[i]) for i,v in enumerate(colCC)],reverse=True)[:10])
# per-token prev preference: fraction of prev-tuned positive weight on V tokens for neurons tuned to that cur token
print('cur token: share of its positive prev tuning on V set, and top-3 prev')
for c in range(28):
    sel=Y[:,c]>2.5
    w=np.clip(z5[sel],0,None)*Y[sel,c][:,None]
    tw=w.sum(0); tw[c]=0
    print(c,'n',sel.sum(),'V share %.2f'%(tw[V].sum()/tw.sum()),[int(i) for i in np.argsort(-tw)[:5]])

VOC="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):

        rest=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',rest); rest=re.sub(r' +',' ',rest).rstrip()
        out.append(lead+rest)
    return '\n'.join(out)
def counts(t):
    t=norm(t); ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t]
    C=np.zeros((28,28))
    for x,y in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[x,y]+=1
    return C
def maxcut(C,restarts=2000,seed=0):
    A=C+C.T; np.fill_diagonal(A,0); tot=A.sum()/2
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); best=(0,None)
    for r in range(restarts):
        s=rng.choice([-1,1],28)
        imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            gain=s*(A@s)      # flipping i changes cut by gain[i]
            i=np.argmax(gain)
            if gain[i]>1e-9: s[i]*=-1; imp=True
        cut=(A*(1-np.outer(s,s))/2).sum()/2
        if cut>best[0]: best=(cut,s.copy())
    return best[0]/tot,best[1]

# null: shuffle rows of the observed

Cp=(np.clip(z5-2,0,None).T@np.clip(Y-2,0,None)); np.fill_diagonal(Cp,0)

ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
tau/=np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau.T
P=W['W_proj.3']; U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False); Ud=U[:,1:28]
Ms={j:Ud.T@oh(j,3)@(hd('W_v',j,3)*D3)@tx for j in [1,5]}
n=lambda A:A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)
O=n(Ms[1])+n(Ms[5])
for lam in [0.3,1.0,3.0]:
    Wout=O.T@np.linalg.inv(O@O.T+lam*np.eye(27))
    Votes=Wout@Ud.T@P            # 28 x 1536
    Votes=Votes-Votes.mean(0,keepdims=True)
    Cv=np.corrcoef(Votes)
    print('lam',lam,'top pairs',sorted([(round(float(Cv[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:10])
    print('   bottom',sorted([(round(float(Cv[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)])[:8])
    u_,s_,vt_=np.linalg.svd(Votes,full_matrices=False)
    print('   vote sv',s_[:8].round(0))
    for r in range(3):
        o=np.argsort(u_[:,r]); print('   PC',r,'neg',[int(i) for i in o[:7]],'pos',[int(i) for i in o[-7:][::-1]])
V=[0,3,7,15,17,25]; C=[i for i in range(28) if i not in V]
Wout=O.T@np.linalg.inv(O@O.T+1.0*np.eye(27)); Votes=Wout@Ud.T@P; Votes=Votes-Votes.mean(0,keepdims=True); Cv=np.corrcoef(Votes)
iu=np.triu_indices(6,1)
print('within-V mean corr',Cv[np.ix_(V,V)][iu].mean().round(3))
print('V matrix');print(Cv[np.ix_(V,V)])

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg
Fa=F[act]; P5=oh(5)@hd('W_v',5)@Th.T@tau.T
Y5=Fa@P5; Y5=Y5-Y5.mean(0)
SS=Y5.T@Y; np.fill_diagonal(SS,0)
Cc=np.corrcoef(SS.T); Cr=np.corrcoef(SS)
print('cols similar to 5:',[(int(i),round(float(Cc[5,i]),2)) for i in np.argsort(-Cc[5])[1:6]])
print('cols similar to 20:',[(int(i),round(float(Cc[20,i]),2)) for i in np.argsort(-Cc[20])[1:6]])
print('top col-sim pairs',sorted([(round(float(Cc[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:14])
print('top row-sim pairs',sorted([(round(float(Cr[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:14])
# neurons co-tuned to pairs of cur tokens: count |Y|>2 in both, positive
Co=((Y>2).T.astype(int)@(Y>2).astype(int)); np.fill_diagonal(Co,0)
print('co-tuned cur token pairs',sorted([(int(Co[i,j]),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:20])
print('co-tuning partners of 5',[(int(i),int(Co[5,i])) for i in np.argsort(-Co[5])[:6]],' of 20',[(int(i),int(Co[20,i])) for i in np.argsort(-Co[20])[:6]])

import numpy as np, warnings

from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
D0=W['ln1_w.0']
S=np.vstack([hd('W_v',1),hd('W_v',5)])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(S,full_matrices=False); Th=vt[:28]
Tb=(Th/D0)
F=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
R=F@Tb.T
rn=np.linalg.norm(R,axis=1); act=rn>4
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Y=ica.fit_transform(R[act]); tau=ica.components_.copy()
sg=np.sign(skew(Y,axis=0)); tau=tau*sg[:,None]; Y=Y*sg

print('top (col+row)/2 pairs',sorted([(round(float(Both[i,j]),2),round(float(Cc[i,j]),2),round(float(Cr[i,j]),2),i,j) for i in range(28) for j in range(i+1,28)],reverse=True)[:14])

# Output-level pair similarity from copy map, and input embedding similarity from Q,K,V used earlier
tau2=tau/np.linalg.norm(tau,axis=1,keepdims=True)
tx=Tb.T@tau2.T

## Call 50

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
hd=lambda n,j,l=0: W[f'{n}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
oh=lambda j,l=0: W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
# position channel: top-2 output dirs of L0H4 (uniform head, reads position subspace)
A=oh(4)@hd('W_v',4); U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); ch=U[:,:2]
print('L0H4 OV sv',S[:4].round(1))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    D=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']; b=W[f'ln1_b.{l}']
    print('layer',l)
    for j in range(6):
        Q=hd('W_q',j,l);K=hd('W_k',j,l)
        kch=(K*D)@ch                      # 64x2 key read of channel
        q0=Q@b
        gain=np.linalg.norm(q0@kch)/8     # constant-query coupling to the channel
        print(f' H{j} |K on channel| {np.linalg.norm(kch):5.1f}  |Q on channel| {np.linalg.norm((Q*D)@ch):4.1f}  const-query coupling {gain:5.2f}  |Q|{np.linalg.norm(Q*D):5.1f} |K|{np.linalg.norm(K*D):5.1f}  tr(QtK)/(|Q||K|) {np.trace((Q*D)@(K*D).T)/(np.linalg.norm(Q*D)*np.linalg.norm(K*D)):+.3f}')

```
